look at the situation properly, and put yourself on another hypothetical. The train is not the good way out. I\u0027ve seen many train accidents live and they\u0027re not pretty and the victim dies a very painful death. You won\u0027t just get splattered into a pancake, you won\u0027t just be split in half or you might not even have the time for your head to get under the tracks. And the worst part of it is you\u0027ll be conscious for all of it up until you die with blood in your throat from either the impact of the train, or the split torso; your final breath with will be filled with blood and a pulsating headache. \n\n2 weeks is not a very small but not a very large time frame either. I advise you to acknowledge your own feelings and try to explain why your body is feeling these feelings you currently are feeling. \nWhy? You\u0027re feeling this immense pressure, this weight. This isn\u0027t a simple decision. This is your brain running a recursive feedback loop. You feel a pang of depression—a hormonal dip—and your mind, seeking a reason, latches onto the grandest, most dramatic explanation: \"I must want to die.| This thought then validates the feeling, which in turn deepens the thought. You\u0027re stuck in a logical loop created by a physical feeling. The challenge isn\u0027t to act on the conclusion, but to break the loop itself. Your body is creating a feeling, and your mind is telling a story to match it. What if the story is wrong?\r\n\r\n\r\n[23/06/2025 10:16 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nGive yourself some more time to process and think about the journey this feeling will lead you to. zipherious\r\n🤔\r\n\r\n\r\n[15/07/2025 4:56 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nwell yeah, it\u0027s a matter of expectation vs reality. the more public your awareness is, the more performative you get. You don\u0027t want other\u0027s to get the wrong idea about you right? people expect you to be normal, so you perform. \nonce you get familiar with someone, the phase of familiarity; it\u0027s more comfortable to be real with the person you\u0027re with because you know the person who is with you will accept you and you will accept them for being real. THIS is where the battle of your Expectation with the reality collides. You\u0027re no longer the author of your own expectations of that single person - you\u0027re now adapting to that person and he is adapting to you.\r\n\r\n\r\n[15/07/2025 4:57 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nez\r\n\r\n\r\n[20/07/2025 5:08 pm] zipherious\r\nStfu\r\n\r\n\r\n[22/07/2025 6:42 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nI just realized Autism is opposite of being wise. \nLet me explain. \nSo it\u0027s known that autists have been known to have trouble with social awareness and communication and basically anything that isn\u0027t up in their interest. \nLet\u0027s suppose: \n1. You can be genetically wise. This means you don\u0027t lack the directional focus for you to get a grasp of your own life and can focus on individual things without undermining your basic needs at a subconscious level.\n2. You can learn wisdom. This is separate from genetic wisdom and requires a lot of guidance, commitment from parents for you to be able to function on your own at all times. Sure you might have needed time to adjust to what you want and what you need, but ultimately it\u0027s similar to being genetically wise. \n3. Autism is:\n 3.1. A genetic neurological mutation, quite frankly has been a parallel to our societal lives. Where since at birth the child was unable to focus on what they\u0027re essentially forced to focus on, at the survival level. This focus, or rather lack thereof, because a certain topic doesn\u0027t motivate them to think about it, pairs them well with diagnosing them with ADHD, which is not entirely false (but I reckon that nobody has ADHD in a sense, it\u0027s just that the attention they need for a certain priority is misplaced) but puts them categorically worse compared to their peers. \n3.2. A lack of interest towards multiple subjects, or more concretely a lazer focus towards their own unshakable topic. \n3.3. It\u0027s just parents fault not being able to teach their kid what to think about, how to sort priorities and whatnot.\r\n\r\n\r\n[22/07/2025 6:42 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nWith that said, it is a spectrum. Everybody is born different, and everybody was raised different. \nI can argue, that high functioning autists are statistically more prone to high functioning autism because they lack inner guidance or that parental figure didn\u0027t etch out their path towards their self-completeness. \nI can argue, Autism is a brain\u0027s way to take a shortcut to whatever subject it wants to focus on. \nI can argue, that low functioning autists are statistically more likely to be a result of genetical mutations in their neurological pathways, or arguably lack thereof to judge their own path without undermining their basic needs or ever higher needs. \nI can argue, Wisdom is an antonym of Autism in this regard. But same with Autism, wisdom is also a spectrum and I can\u0027t expect everyone to be wise in the same sense anyone can be autistic. \n\nI can conclude that Autism is a misplacement of attention, and wisdom is the art of organisation of such.\r\n\r\n\r\n[22/07/2025 7:48 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n[22/07/2025 7:48 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nit\u0027s sufficient zipherious\r\nMaybe?\r\n\r\n\r\n[05/08/2025 8:41 pm] zipherious\r\nThe void it speaks\nIt speaks of the words \nUnfathomable chords\nWhich the thought of often leaks\n\nMaybe its a blessing\nBut i say its a curse\nAt this point im just guessing\nBut thats making it worse\n\nI would like to see\nI would like to feel \nBut with the thought of me\nI dont wanna deal\n\nI was getting better \nBut the air was getting thin\nAll of it was a pharse \nEverything a lie\nThe fake laughter\nThe fake sense of happiness \nI thought it was over \nYet the air is getting colder\n\nThe smile i once had \nThe darkness ripped it out\nIt made me question \nIt made me scream out loud\n\nThis isnt ending but\nI want it to be over \nI want it to be over\nI want it all to end\nI want to be happy \nI want to be loved\nI want...\nI want to be seen\nYet i feel thats just a dream\nNothing ever mattered\n\nAs i fall deeper in the void\nThe silence deafens even the smallest noise\nThis fall is never ending \nThe fall was never meant to end\nIt will always just continue \nThere never will be any last stand\n\nI have nothing\nI am nothing\nI am not worthless\nI just dont exist\n\nSo why do i still breathe? \nWhy do i have to think? \nWhy do i have to want, \nFor anything? \n\nI questioned my life\ndeeper i fell \nThe faster i fell\nIts getting dark\nIts getting foggy\nIts becoming hard to breathe\nGoodbye now \nBut you dont care \nBecouse i never fit.\r\n\r\n\r\n[05/08/2025 8:41 pm] zipherious\r\nTell me what you think of it\r\n\r\n\r\n[05/08/2025 8:59 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\n**\"thought of often\"** is poetic, you should have it as a poetic phrase. \nSo:\nI would like to see\nI would like to feel \nBut with the ~~thought of me~~ thought of often\nI dont wanna deal\n\nI don\u0027t wanna deal is informal\nMake it \u003eI don\u0027t want a/this deal. \n\nAll of it was a pharse \nEverything a lie\n\u003e\u003e All of it is a phrase, \n\u003e\u003e a lie. (all of it \u003d everything)\n\u003e\u003e Fake laughter, \n\u003e\u003e sense of happiness, (it adds more impact if you sensed it was happiness rather than the feeling of it being fake)...\n\nThe smile i once had \nThe darkness ripped it out\n\u003e\u003e I once had a smile, now masked by darkness,\n\u003e\u003e It questioned, it screamed,\n\u003e\u003e Why such smile, \n\u003e\u003e if I\u0027m a mess?  (or some shit)\n\nNext verse:\n\nThis isnt ending but\nfor it to be over,\nI want it to be over,\nto end.\nbe happy.\nbe loved,\nI want...\nI want to be seen\nFeels just like a dream\nNothing has ever mattered\n\nFalling deeper unto the void, \nsilence is deafening, \nbut the smallest noise\nnever falls to its end. \nIt will always persist,\nlast stand here will never be.\n\n\n\n\nNotes:\n- try to omit repetition (althought repitition can be poetic), adds flavour and more interpetational freedom.\n- write in the present tense, unless you\u0027re building a story. \n- use punctuations to make your poem rhythmical. \n- try to use more encapsulating words that replace entire sentences. (Like \"I feel very empty inside\" -\u003e Melancholy dissolved me or some shit)\n-\r\n\r\n\r\n[05/08/2025 9:00 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\n(still editing)\r\n\r\n\r\n[05/08/2025 9:07 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nYour poem feels and in my opinion is disconnected with itself. —\nDo I belong?\nIt is why I am unencumbered.\nIt’s why I am writing this wretched song.\n\nNo one heard my screams —\nThe screams, they echoed in the night.\nAll they saw was a remnant of a dream,\nA dream about my last fight.\n\nI can’t go on like this.\nI can’t do this anymore.\nI want to not miss —\nMiss the things I had before.\n\nThis isn’t changing anytime soon.\nOn my chest, my heart — there’s a wound.\nIt cannot heal, but fuck it hurts.\n\nI wanted to be noticed and seen.\nI wanted to be respected and loved.\nAll I got was hits of dopamine,\nBut I never was beloved.\n\nNow I’m waiting — that things will change.\nNow I’m waiting, hopeless and in pain.\nThis has to end, this deranged —\n’Cause all the waiting was in vain.\n\nAhh, I think it’s time to say goodbye now.\nThe train is coming down the track.\nThis is the last time I sigh.\nGoodbye now — don’t wait. You\u0027re saying that your Cs aren\u0027t equal or bigger than the L you experienced, but the only way you\u0027re feeling you could solve this is with is an S, which stands bigger and/or equal to C and L. \n\n - So you\u0027d be saying S\u003eL\u003eC and that S could solve your problems because S towers over L and C. \n\n2. positive impacts are prefixed with + and negative with -, in this case -L would be considered negative, +L would have given you the opposite of -L, which is the antonym of loss, in your case. \n\n3. C - (-L) \u003e\u003d0  \n\u003e but this isn\u0027t the case, because the C isn\u0027t as big as -(-L). \n\nYou spiral with your thoughts onto the lateral space and think of a solution that could solve your problems, S (valued -S) \nSO: (-S) - (-L) \u003e\u003d 0 \nbut here\u0027s the problem. \n\nthere isn\u0027t a positive S or you\u0027d literally have to invent resurrection for this to be true at certain times, this equation will never hold true either. \n\u003e No matter how small your -S would have been, the outcome would have never been positive, neither for you, nor for anybody around you. \n\nProposal: \nYou find a meaningful C, that will successfully counteract the L. \nIf the previous statement doesn\u0027t hold true, find another variable to find meaning in other places\nelse if, you live with it\r\n\r\n\r\n[10/08/2025 7:30 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nAnd by finding another variable, I mean something new that will give you a positive outcome at another topic, another genre, another book in your life\u0027s chapter.\r\n\r\n\r\n[10/08/2025 7:31 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nand by living with it, is by living with the negative effects you are having right now, learning from it would make it so that eventually you\u0027ll turn this equation positive.\r\n\r\n\r\n[10/08/2025 7:34 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nexcuse me for the paragraphs :3\r\n\r\n\r\n[10/08/2025 8:19 pm] zipherious\r\nI like what your tryna do but the thing is S is neither a negative neither a pozitive neither a zero it\u0027s  like a restart button or turn off button for when you either:\n1. 0_____________________________.0\r\npapayapa \u003c3\r\n\r\n\r\n[31/08/2025 3:12 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nI\u0027d like to propose a new word: infermation; derivement from the word information, which is a word for a statement or a set of meaningful words that give and provide meaning or description of a certain, object; often set within the informational context. \nInfermation would be a derivement from information because it would look at the reason of meaning behind the \u0027being\u0027 rather than attributing value of meaning, we can attribute value of reason along with the contextual confidence score that usually might go along with infermation. \nInfermation solves a lot of ambiguity and accusatory language semanticism in terms of \u0027attacking\u0027 or arguing with another person, that would usually leave the other person vurnerable with the information he\u0027d be accused of.\nWith infermation against you, you\u0027d be inferred to be the \"inferred\", e.g. like labels of pleading homophobia when you\u0027re in a debate against gay relationship, when in fact there is no plausible evidence that would suggest that.\nInfermation forces people to think about information and infermation itself whether an argument is being supported by informational reasoning (research with scientific evidence or reliable sources) or infermational leaps of judgement (jumping to conclusions) made by the inferrent.\r\n\r\n\r\n[31/08/2025 5:49 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nlmk your thoughts \u003c3\r\n\r\n\r\n[31/08/2025 8:59 pm] zipherious\r\nso basicaly The darkness which hadnt had the chance to fully engulf the body of a wrecked man, slowly crept as the sun started setting.\nIt was a mostly peaceful end of the day, as birds chirped and the churched bells sang \"the time is six o\u0027clock\", as the familes gathered for dinner, as the stores made their last receipt. \nIt was peaceful for most but one man. That man was slowly, nearly crawling trough the streets of a tired town. The streets, which in about 2 hours will be filled with life, as the night brings out the cheerful celebration for the end of the week, as the cuples will start populating restaurants, filling the air with joy and love. The man had a rough day, but then again every day is rough for that man, as the work withers his limbs, slowly tearing his mind apart, as the thought of going home to his \"loving\" family, though he knows that that love is only rewarded by screams and an eternal feeling of loneliness.\nThe man intoxicates him self every day just to keep himself sane, as the ethanol rots away his mind and leaves him even more depressed as he was. \nThe man had enough, as he walks past the bar the house he once called home, as he aproaches the forest which slowly pulls him into its hug not planing to let go. \nThe trees sing, as the faint wind passes through the branches, as the leaves swish around making that nostalgic rustling noise, as the man walks through.\nHe thinks that his life was meaningless, as noone really cared for him, as noone ever truly loved him, as the only thing that made him happy was the thought of this night. \nTick tock went the clock as the body of a wrecked man was engulfed in darkness, as the rope tightened from the pulls of hungry wolves, as the once happy man hanged from a happy tree.\r\n\r\n\r\n[14/11/2025 2:15 pm] zipherious\r\nWacha think\r\n\r\n\r\n[14/11/2025 3:37 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\nThis is what i think.\nThe story is compact and rich with narrative, man grieving (for an unapparent reason), metaphorically challenging, rich with the feelings you tried to embed it with. \nHowever, given this is the first prose I\u0027m reading from you, I can tell you a few improvements you need to make. \nYou\u0027re struggling with the \"show not tell\" narrative. You fail to show the feelings portrayed by the man with the specifics of his surroundings that (i may add) are irrelevant to the whole narrative of the story. The prose reads more like a diary, rather than something with flow, with intent or motivation to impose a feeling. There are moments of awkward phrasing like \"the bells sang \"the time is six o\u0027clock\"\".\n You get very specific within the narrative down to ethanol, which replaces the man\u0027s beverage. This closes the gap for interpretation for the reader. \nMoments like \"The trees sing, as the faint wind passes...\" in contrast to \"The man had enough, as he walks past...\" is an apparent contrast between the narrative. \"2 hours will be filled with life\" feels kind of redundant and can be easily replaced with something less descriptive, like e.g.: lonely morning street, or before the break of dawn. \n\nYour prose screams for ethereality, so provide ethereal descriptors. \nAll in all, it\u0027s a great opportunity to open yourself for something more expressive, rather than limit yourself with poems that fit the poems\u0027 frame.\r\n\r\n\r\n[14/11/2025 7:00 pm] 0_____________________________.0\r\n\r\n\r\n{Attachments}\r\nhttps://cdn.discordapp.com/attachments/1342566851837952081/1438951688332640339/Screenshot_20251114_190009.jpg?ex\u003d69818ef0\u0026is\u003d69803d70\u0026hm\u003de79e28b210e6659eeb48beaaae0ff7009a72f458da9d2ab0397b2de05f1cf1e9\u0026\r\n\r\n\r\n[14/11/2025 7:02 pm] zipherious\r\nthanks for the insight i will see what i can do for mz writing to improve ill keep you updated :3\r\n\r\n\r\n[14/11/2025 7:02 pm] zipherious\r\nim stealing this\r\n\r\n\r\n[17/11/2025 12:26 pm] zipherious\r\nI just had the wierdest saddest dream i ever had\r\n\r\n\r\n[17/11/2025 12:45 pm] zipherious\r\nMe and i think it was 5sg were toogether and i went to his place and there was everyone as he had a brother and a sister but the brother was you... (x×x×x×...×x){n times}×(y×y×...×y){n times} holds equivalence with (xy×xy×...×xy){n times} because \nat base value; n \u003d 1: xy\u003dyx \u003d\u003e x¹×y¹\u003d(xy)¹ \nso considering the initial statement\nso for all cases of n, and for n+1:\nso proving (xy)^(n+1) \u003d x^(n+1) × y^(n+1) therefore we can rewrite it as:\n(xy)^n × xy \u003d x^n × x × y^n × y\nand since we can induce from the base that x × y \u003d xy we can group the right hand side like:\n(xy)^n × (xy)¹ \u003d (xy)^(n+1)\nwe can see it holds for all cases.\r\n\r\n\r\n[20/01/2026 9:04 am] zipherious\r\nmy indecicive bitch
